# Ajitasenavyākaraṇa (The Prediction of Ajitasena)

1.1 oṃ namaḥ sarvajñāya ||  
?(oṃ) 🚻⨀①(namas) 🚹⨀④🆎(sarva+jña)  
oṃ | homage | to the All Knowing  
Oṃ, homage to the `sarvajña` (All Knowing).

2.1 evaṃ mayā śrutam ekasmin samaye bhagavān* śrāvastyāṃ viharati sma jetavane anāthapiṇḍadasyārāme mahatā bhikṣusaṃghena sārdham ardhatrayodaśabhir bhikṣusahasraiḥ |  
⏏️(evam) *️⃣👆⚧⨀③(asmad) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(śruta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑦(eka) 🚹⨀⑦(samaya) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) 🚺⨀②🔽(ya)(śrāvastya) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(viharati) ⏏️(sma) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(jeta+vana) 🚹⨀⑥(anāthapiṇḍada) 🚹⨀⑦(ārāma) 🚹⨀③(mahat) 🚹⨀③🆎(bhikṣu+saṃgha) ⏏️(sārdham) 🚹⨂③🆎(ardha+trayodaśan) 🚻⨂③🆎(bhikṣu+sa+hasra)  
thus | by me | heard | at one | at a time | Bhagavān | in Śrāvastī | dwells | (past) | in Jetavana | of Anāthapiṇḍada | in the park | with a great | with the assembly of bhikṣus | together with | with twelve and a half | with thousands of bhikṣus  
Thus heard by me. At one time Bhagavān was dwelling at Śrāvastī in the Jetavana, in the park of Anāthapiṇḍada, together with a great saṃgha of bhikṣus, with twelve thousand five hundred bhikṣus,

2.2 tadyathā āyuṣmatā cājñātakauṇḍinyena āyuṣmatā ca mahānāmnā āyuṣmatā ca revatena āyuṣmatā ca vakkulena āyuṣmatā ca śāripu[treṇa āyuṣmatā ca] pūrṇena maitrāyaṇī[putreṇa] ca śrāvakaniyutaiḥ |  
⏏️🆎(tad+yathā) 🚹⨀③(āyuṣmat) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(ājñātakauṇḍinya+ina) 🚹⨀③(āyuṣmat) ⏏️(ca) 🚻⨀③🆎(mahā+nāman) 🚹⨀③(āyuṣmat) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(revata+ina) 🚹⨀③(āyuṣmat) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①🆎(vakkula+ina) 🚹⨀③(āyuṣmat) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①(śa) 🚻⨀①(ripu) 🚹⨀③(tra) 🚹⨀③(āyuṣmat) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③🔽(ta)(pūrṇa) 🚺⨀①(maitrāyaṇī) 🚹⨀③(putra) ⏏️(ca) 🚻⨂③🔽(ta)🆎(śrāvaka+niyuta)  
namely | with āyuṣmān | and | with Ājñātakauṇḍinya | with āyuṣmān | and | with Mahānāman | with āyuṣmān | and | with Revata | with āyuṣmān | and | with Vakkula | with āyuṣmān | and | Śāri- | -ripu- | with -putra | with āyuṣmān | and | with Pūrṇa | Maitrāyaṇī | with the son | and | with millions of disciples  
namely, āyuṣmān Ājñātakauṇḍinya, āyuṣmān Mahānāman, āyuṣmān Revata, āyuṣmān Vakkula, āyuṣmān Śāriputra, and āyuṣmān Pūrṇa Maitrāyaṇīputra, along with niyutas of śrāvakas.

2.3 te sarve yena bhagavān yena ca jetavanaṃ vihāraṃ tenopasaṃkrāntā bhagavataḥ pādau śirasābhivandya bhagavataḥ puratas tasthur dvātriṃśatā bodhisatvasahasraiḥ |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(tad) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(sarva) ⏏️(yena) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) ⏏️(yena) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(jeta+vana) 🚹⨀②(vihāra) ⏏️(tena) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(upasaṃkrānta) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) 🚹⨁②(pāda) 🚻⨀③(śiras) 🔽(ya)(abhivandate) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) ⏏️(puratas) 🟢⨂🤟🔄(tiṣṭhati) 🚺⨀③(dvātriṃśat) 🚻⨂③🆎(bodhi+sattva+sa+hasra)  
they | all | where | Bhagavān | where | and | Jetavana | monastery | there | approached | of Bhagavān | two feet | with the head | having saluted | of Bhagavān | in front | stood | with thirty-two | with thousands of bodhisattvas  
All of them approached where Bhagavān was and where the Jetavana monastery was; having bowed with their heads at the feet of Bhagavān, they stood before Bhagavān together with thirty-two thousand bodhisattvas,

2.4 tadyathā sahacittotpādadharmacakrapravartanena ca bodhisatvena mahāsatvena anikṣiptadhureṇa ca bodhisatvena mahāsatvena maitreyeṇa ca bodhisatvena mahāsatvena avalokiteśvareṇa ca bodhisatvena mahāsatvena mahāsthāmaprāptena ca bodhisatvena mahāsatvena |  
⏏️🆎(tad+yathā) 🚹⨀③🆎(saha+citta+utpāda+dharma+cakra+pravartana) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③(bodhisattva) 🚹⨀③(mahāsattva) 🚹⨀③🆎(na+nikṣipta+dhura) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③(bodhisattva) 🚹⨀③(mahāsattva) 🚹⨀③(maitreya) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③(bodhisattva) 🚹⨀③(mahāsattva) 🚻⨀③🆎(avalokita+īśvara) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③(bodhisattva) 🚹⨀③(mahāsattva) 🚹⨀①🆎(mahāsthāmaprāpta+ina) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀③(bodhisattva) 🚹⨀③(mahāsattva)  
namely | with Sahacittotpādadharmacakrapravartana | and | with the bodhisattva | with the mahāsattva | with Anikṣiptadhura | and | with the bodhisattva | with the mahāsattva | with Maitreya | and | with the bodhisattva | with the mahāsattva | with Avalokiteśvara | and | with the bodhisattva | with the mahāsattva | with Mahāsthāmaprāpta | and | with the bodhisattva | with the mahāsattva  
namely, the bodhisattva mahāsattva Sahacittotpādadharmacakrapravartana, the bodhisattva mahāsattva Anikṣiptadhura, the bodhisattva mahāsattva Maitreya, the bodhisattva mahāsattva Avalokiteśvara, and the bodhisattva mahāsattva Mahāsthāmaprāpta.

2.5 evaṃpramukhair dvātriṃśatā bodhisatvasahasraiḥ |  
🚻⨂③🆎(evam+pramukha) 🚺⨀③(dvātriṃśat) 🚻⨂③🆎(bodhi+sattva+sa+hasra)  
with those headed by these | with thirty-two | with thousands of bodhisattvas  
With thirty-two thousand bodhisattvas headed by these,

2.6 te sarve yena bhagavān yena ca jetavanaṃ vihāraṃ tenopasaṃkrāntā  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(tad) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(sarva) ⏏️(yena) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) ⏏️(yena) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀②🆎(jeta+vana) 🚹⨀②(vihāra) ⏏️(tena) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(upasaṃkrānta)  
they | all | where | Bhagavān | where | and | Jetavana | monastery | there | approached  
all of them approached where Bhagavān was and where the Jetavana monastery was.

3.1 bhagavataḥ pādau śirasābhivandya bhagavataḥ puratas tasthuḥ |  
🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) 🚹⨁②(pāda) 🚻⨀③(śiras) 🔽(ya)(abhivandate) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) ⏏️(puratas) 🟢⨂🤟🔄(tiṣṭhati)  
of Bhagavān | two feet | with the head | having saluted | of Bhagavān | in front | stood  
Having bowed with their heads at the feet of Bhagavān, they stood before Bhagavān.

3.2 atha khalu bhagavān pūrvāhṇakālasamaye nivāsya pātracīvaramā[traḥ] śrāvastīṃ mahānagarīṃ piṇḍāya prāviśat* |  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(khalu) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(pūrva) 🚺⨀①(āhnaka) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+lasa) 🚹⨀④(e) 🔽(ya)(nivāsayati) ?(pātracīvaramā) 🚹⨀①(tra) 🚺⨀②(śrāvastī) 🚺⨀②🆎(mahā+nagarī) 🚹⨀④(piṇḍa) 🟢⨀🤟↩️(praviśati)  
then | indeed | Bhagavān | fore- | -noon | time | at the occasion | having dressed | bowl and robe | only | Śrāvastī | great city | for alms | entered  
Then indeed Bhagavān, having dressed in the forenoon time, taking only his bowl and robe, entered the great city of Śrāvastī for alms.

3.3 atha bhagavān āyuṣmantam ānandam (Asv 104) āmantrayate sma |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) 🚹⨀②(āyuṣmat) 🚹⨀②(ānand) 🔵⨀🤟▶️(āmantrati) ⏏️(sma)  
then | Bhagavān | āyuṣmān | Ānanda | addresses | (past)  
Then Bhagavān addressed āyuṣmān Ānanda:

3.4 gacchānanda pātraṃ cakrikaṃ śikyamānaya |  
🚹⨀①🆎(gaccha+ānanda) 🚻⨀②(pātra) 🚻⨀②(cakrika) 🚻⨀②(śikya) 🟢⨀🤘⏹(ānayati)  
Gacchānanda | bowl | ring | carrying sling | bring  
"Go, Ānanda, bring the bowl, the ring, and the `śikya` (carrying sling)."

3.5 athāyuṣmān ānando bhagavataḥ śrutamātreṇa pātraṃ cakrikaṃ śikyaṃ bhagavate upanāmayām āsa |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(āyuṣmat) 🟢⨀🤘↩️(ānandati) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) 🚻⨀③🆎(śruta+mātra) 🚻⨀②(pātra) 🚻⨀②(cakrika) 🚻⨀②(śikya) 🚹⨀④(bhagavat) 🚹⨀①(upanā) 🚺⨀②🔽(maya)🆎(na+maya) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(asati)  
then | āyuṣmān | Ānanda | of Bhagavān | by merely hearing | bowl | ring | carrying sling | to Bhagavān | presented | (part of presented) | (auxiliary past)  
Then āyuṣmān Ānanda, as soon as he heard from Bhagavān, presented the bowl, the ring, and the śikya to Bhagavān.

3.6 athāyuṣmān ānando bhagavataḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhagavantaṃ gāthābhir adhyabhāṣata |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(āyuṣmat) 🟢⨀🤘↩️(ānandati) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) 🚹⨀①🆎(kṛtāñjali+puṭa) 🚹⨀②(bhagavat) 🚺⨂③(gāthā) 🟢⨀🤟↩️(adhibhāṣate)  
then | āyuṣmān | Ānanda | of Bhagavān | with joined palms | Bhagavān | with verses | addressed  
Then āyuṣmān Ānanda, with palms joined before Bhagavān, addressed Bhagavān in `gāthā` (verses).

4.1 yadā tvaṃ praviśasi piṇḍapātika  
⏏️(yadā) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(yuṣmad) 🟢⨀🤘▶️(praviśati) 🚹⨀⓪🆎(piṇḍa+pātika)  
when | you | enter | alms-food collector  
When you enter, `piṇḍapātika` (alms-food collector).

5.1 vimocaye tvaṃ bahavaṃ hi prāṇinām* /–MS: vimocayeyaṃ ...  
🟢⨀👆▶️(vimocayati) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(yuṣmad) ?(bahavaṃ) ⏏️(hi) 🚹⨂⑥(prāṇin) ?(MS) : 🟢⨀👆⏯(vimocayati)  
may you liberate | you | many | indeed | of living beings | manuscript | : | may I liberate  
Indeed you release many breathing beings.

5.2 uttārayeyaṃ  
🟢⨀👆⏯(uttārayati)  
may I ferry across  
May I ferry across.

6.1 uttāraye tvaṃ bahavaṃ satvā  
🟢⨀👆▶️(uttārayati) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(yuṣmad) ?(bahavaṃ) 🚹⨀①(satvan)  
may you ferry across | you | many | beings  
You carry across many beings.

7.1 narakabhayāj jātijarāmahābhayā //  
🚻⨀⑤🆎(naraka+bhaya) 🚺⨀①🆎(jāti+jarā+mahā+bhaya)  
from the fear of hell | from the great fear of birth and old age  
From the fear of hell, from the great fear of `jāti` (birth) and old age.

8.1 saṃsāraduḥkhakalilā mahābhayād  
🚺⨀①🆎(saṃsāra+duḥkha+kalila) 🚻⨀⑤🆎(mahā+bhaya)  
from the thicket of dissatisfaction of the cycle of rebirth | from great fear  
From the great fear of the tangle of the `duḥkha` (dissatisfaction) of the `saṃsāra` (cycle of rebirth).

9.1 {vimocayeyaṃ tava lokanāyakāḥ} /  
  


9.2 mahānubhāvo varadakṣiṇīyo  
🚹⨀①(mahānubhāva) 🚹⨀①🆎(vara+dakṣiṇīya)  
of mahānubhāva | most worthy of offerings

{vimocayeyaṃ tava lokanāyakāḥ}  
The mahānubhāva, supreme worthy of offerings.

10.1 vimocayitvā {bahavaṃ hi satvā /  
🔽(tvā)(vimocayati) ?(bahavaṃ) ⏏️(hi) 🚹⨀①(satvan)  
having liberated | many | indeed | beings

10.2 saṃsāraduḥkhakalilā mahābhayaḥ  
🚺⨀①🆎(saṃsāra+duḥkha+kalila) 🚹⨀①🆎(mahā+bhaya)  
from the thicket of dissatisfaction of the cycle of rebirth | great fear

Having released indeed many beings.  
From the great fear of the tangle of the dissatisfaction of the cycle of rebirth.

11.1 vimocayitvā} punaraṃ hi āgamī //  
🔽(tvā)(vimocayati) ⏏️(punar) ⏏️(hi) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(āgacchati)  
having liberated | again | indeed | will return  
Having released them, he will indeed return again.

12.1 athāyuṣmān ānando bhagavata imā gāthā bhāṣitvā tūṣṇīṃ sthito 'bhūt* |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(āyuṣmat) 🟢⨀🤘↩️(ānandati) 🚹⨀⑥(bhagavat) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂②(idam) 🚺⨂②(gāthā) 🔽(tvā)(bhāṣate) ⏏️(tūṣṇīm) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sthita) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(bhavati)  
then | āyuṣmān | Ānanda | of Bhagavān | these | verses | having spoken | silently | stood | became  
Then āyuṣmān Ānanda, having spoken these gāthās to Bhagavān, stood silent.

12.2 atha bhagavān* śrāvastyāṃ mahānagaryāṃ nātidūre sthito 'bhūt* |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) 🚺⨀②🔽(ya)(śrāvastya) 🚺⨀②🆎(mahā+nagarī) ⏏️(na) 🚻⨀⑦(atidūra) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sthita) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(bhavati)  
then | Bhagavān | in Śrāvastī | in the great city | not | at a great distance | stood | became  
Then Bhagavān stood not far from the great city of Śrāvastī.

12.3 atha te sarve gavākṣatoraṇaniryūhakā [hiraṇya]mayāḥ sphaṭikamayā rūpyamayāḥ prādurabhūvan* |  
⏏️(atha) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(tad) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(sarva) 🚹⨂①🔽(ka)🆎(gavākṣa+toraṇa+niryūha+ka) 🚻⨀⓪(hiraṇya) 🚹⨂①(maya) 🚹⨂①(sphaṭikamaya) 🚹⨂①(rūpyamaya) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(prādurbhavati)  
then | those | all | windows, arches, and pinnacles | gold | made of | made of crystal | made of silver | appeared  
Then all those round windows, archways, and pinnacles appeared made of gold, made of crystal, and made of silver.

12.4 tathā śrāvastyāṃ ma[hānagaryāṃ] mahāntaṃ janakāyaṃ saṃsthito 'bhūvan* |  
⏏️(tathā) 🚺⨀②🔽(ya)(śrāvastya) *️⃣👆⚧⨀④(asmad) 🚹⨂②(ha) 🚺⨀⑦🆎(na+garī) 🚹⨀②(mahat) 🚹⨀②🆎(jana+kāya) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(saṃsthita) 🟢⨂🤟⏮(bhavati)  
likewise | in Śrāvastī | great- | -great- | in the city | great | crowd of people | assembled | became  
Likewise, in the great city of Śrāvastī, a great crowd of people gathered.

12.5 atha sa janakāyaḥ saṃśayajāto babhūva ko hetuḥ kaḥ pratyayaḥ nagarasya śubhanimittaṃ prādurabhūt* |  
⏏️(atha) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(tad) 🚹⨀①🆎(jana+kāya) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(saṃśaya+jāta) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(bhavati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(kim) 🚹⨀①(hetu) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(kim) 🚹⨀①(pratyaya) 🚻⨀⑥(nagara) 🚻⨀①🆎(śubha+nimitta) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(prādurbhavati)  
then | that | crowd of people | filled with doubt | became | what | primary primary cause | what | cause | of the city | auspicious sign | appeared  
Then that crowd of people became filled with doubt: "What is the `hetu` (primary cause), what is the primary `pratyaya` (cause) that this `śubhanimitta` (auspicious sign) of the city has appeared?

12.6 mā cedaṃ nagaraṃ bhasmapralayaṃ syāt* |  
*️⃣👆⚧⨀②(asmad) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(idam) 🚻⨀①(nagara) 🚻⨀①🆎(bhasman+pralaya) 🟢⨀🤟⏯(asti)  
may not | and | this | city | dissolution into ashes | might become  
May this city not be dissolved into ashes!"

12.7 atha tatra janakāye anekavarṣaśatasahasriko (Asv 105) jīrṇo vṛddho mahallakaḥ puruṣaḥ saṃsthito 'bhūt* |  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(tatra) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(jana+kāya) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+eka+varṣa+śata+sahasrika) 🚹⨀①(jīrṇa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(vṛddha) 🚹⨀①(mahallaka) 🚹⨀①(puruṣa) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(saṃsthita) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(bhavati)  
then | there | in the crowd of people | of many hundreds of thousands of years | aged | elderly | advanced in years | man | standing | was  
Then in that crowd of people stood a decrepit, aged, elderly man of many hundreds of thousands of years.

12.8 atha sa puruṣas taṃ janakāyaṃ samāśvāsayann evam āha |  
⏏️(atha) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(tad) 🚹⨀①(puruṣa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(tad) 🚹⨀②🆎(jana+kāya) 🚹⨀①🔽(at)(samāśvāsayat) ⏏️(evam) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
then | that | man | that | crowd of people | reassuring | thus | said  
Then that man, reassuring that crowd of people, spoke thus:

then | of Bhagavān | by merely entering | in Śrāvastī | in the great city | ninety-nine tens of millions of myriads of hundreds of thousands | of beings | in Sukhāvatī | in the world system system | established | eighty-four tens of millions of myriads of hundreds of thousands of beings | of Abhirati | of the world- | -system | (genitive ending) | of the thus-gone one Akṣobhya | field of a Buddha | established  
Then, as soon as Bhagavān had entered the great city of Śrāvastī, ninety-nine hundred thousand niyutas of tens of millions of `sattva` (beings) were established in the `lokadhātu` (world system) system of Sukhāvatī, and eighty-four hundred thousand niyutas of tens of millions of beings were established in the Buddha field of Tathāgata Akṣobhya in the world system system of Abhirati.

Then Bhagavān, together with Ānanda, arrived at the house of the city-beggar girl.

my | to the house | not | ever | alms-food collector | come | had

31.1 atha sa nagarāvalambikā dārikā śūnyākāragṛhe [niṣaṇṇā] imā gāthā bhāṣitvā tūṣṇīṃ sthitābhūt* |

31.5 atha śuddhavāsakāyiko devaputraḥ śatasahasramūlyaṃ muktāhāraṃ gṛhītvā śatarasabhojanapiṭakaṃ gṛhītvā kāśikāni vastrāṇi gṛhītvā yena nagarāvalambikāyā dārikāyā gṛhaṃ tenopasaṃkrānto 'bhūt* |

Then the deva-son of the Pure Abodes class, taking a pearl necklace worth a hundred thousand, taking a basket of food of a hundred flavours, and taking garments from Kāśī, approached where the house of the city-beggar girl was.

Having put them on, taking this pearl necklace worth a hundred thousand and taking this basket of food of a hundred flavours, present them to Bhagavān."

31.9 atha sā dārikā tāni kāśikāni vastrāṇi prāvṛtya śatasahasramūlyaṃ muktāhāraṃ gṛhītvā taṃ śatarasabhojanapiṭakaṃ gṛhītvā yena bhagavāṃs tenopasaṃkrāntā |

Then that girl, having put on those garments from Kāśī, taking the pearl necklace worth a hundred thousand and taking that basket of food of a hundred flavours, approached where Bhagavān was.

There will be an end to female `bhāva` (existent); there will be an end to a poor house."

32.7 atha sā nagarāvalambikā dārikā taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ pariṇāmayitvā bhagavate datvā svagṛhagamanam ārabdhā |

then | Bhagavān | that | girl | thus | said

"Formerly, `dārike` (girl), in past time, innumerable aeons ago, there was a Tathāgata named Ratnaśikhin, arhan, Samyaksaṃbuddha, endowed with knowledge and conduct, Sugata, lokavid, unsurpassed puruṣadamyasārathi, Śāstṛ of devas and humans, Buddha, Bhagavān.

49.3 tena (Asv 112) khalu punaḥ samayena padmāvatī nāma rājadhāny abhūt* |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(tad) ⏏️(khalu) ⏏️(punar) 🚹⨀③(samaya) 🚺⨀①(padmāvatī) ⏏️(nāma) 🚺⨀①🆎(rāja+dhānī) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(bhavati)  
at that | indeed | again | at the time | Padmāvatī | by name | royal capital | was  
Indeed at that very time there was a royal capital named Padmāvatī.

at that | indeed | moreover | at the time | in Padmāvatī | in the royal capital | Padmaprabha | by name | householder | was  
Indeed at that very time in the royal capital of Padmāvatī there was a householder named Padmaprabhu.

49.5 tasya padmaprabhasya gṛhapates tvaṃ [duhi]tābhūḥ |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(tad) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(padma+prabha) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(gṛha+pati) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(yuṣmad) 🚺⨀⑦(duh) 🚺⨀①(tā) 🟢⨀🤘⏮(bhavati)  
of that | of Padmaprabha | of the householder | you | daughter | she | were  
You were the daughter of that householder Padmaprabhu.

49.6 tena khalu punaḥ samayena grāmanagaranigamajanapadeṣu piṇḍapātiko bhikṣuḥ piṇḍapātāyāvatarati |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(tad) ⏏️(khalu) ⏏️(punar) 🚹⨀③(samaya) 🚹⨂⑦🆎(grāma+nagara+nigama+jana+pada) 🚹⨀①🆎(piṇḍa+pātika) 🚹⨀①(bhikṣu) 🚹⨀④🆎(piṇḍa+pāta) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(avatarati)

Indeed at that very time a piṇḍapāta-food collector bhikṣu was going round the villages, towns, market-towns, and districts for piṇḍapāta.

49.7 yadā tvadgṛham āgato 'bhūt tadā tvaṃ dārike piṇḍapātaṃ gṛhītvā gṛhān niṣkrāntā punar eva praviṣṭābhūḥ |  
⏏️(yadā) 🚻⨀②🆎(tvad+gṛha) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(āgata) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(bhavati) ⏏️(tadā) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(yuṣmad) 🚺⨀⓪(dārikā) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṇḍa+pāta) 🔽(tvā)(gṛhṇāti) 🚻⨀⑤(gṛha) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(niṣkrānta) ⏏️(punar) ⏏️(eva) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(praviṣṭa) 🟢⨀🤘⏮(bhavati)  
when | your house | arrived | was | then | you | dārikā rent, chink | alms-food-food | having taken | from the house | gone out | again | indeed | entered | were  
When he came to your house, then you, `dārike` (girl), having taken piṇḍapāta and stepped out of the house, went straight back inside again, saying,

49.8 na cāhaṃ muṇḍitaśiraso 'dhanyasya piṇḍapātaṃ dāsyāmi |  
⏏️(na) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(asmad) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(muṇḍita+śiras) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(na+dhanya) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṇḍa+pāta) 🟢⨀👆⏭(dadāti)  
not | and | I | to the shaven-headed one | to the unfortunate one | alms-food-food | will give  
'And I will not give piṇḍapāta to an unlucky shaven-head.'

49.9 tena karmopacayena tvayā dārike dvādaśa kalpasahasrāṇi punaḥ punar daridragṛhe duḥkhāny anubhūtāni |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀③(tad) 🚹⨀③🆎(karman+upacaya) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀③(yuṣmad) 🚺⨀⓪(dārikā) 🚻⨂①(dvādaśan) 🚻⨂②🆎(kalpa+sa+hasra) ⏏️(punar) ⏏️(punar) 🚻⨀⑦🆎(daridra+gṛha) 🚻⨂①(duḥkha) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)(anubhūta)  
by that | by the accumulation of action | by you | dārikā rent, chink | twelve | thousands of aeons | again | again | in a poor house | dissatisfactions | experienced  
Through that accumulation of action, `dārike` (girl), for twelve thousand aeons you have experienced duḥkhas again and again in a poor house.

49.10 ekena tvayā  
*️⃣🤟🚻⨀③(eka) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀③(yuṣmad)

50.1 dārike kuśalamūlena bodhivyākaraṇaṃ pratilapsyase |  
🚺⨀⓪(dārikā) 🚻⨀③🆎(kuśala+mūla) 🚻⨀②🆎(bodhi+vyākaraṇa) 🔵⨀🤘⏭(pratilabhate)  
dārikā rent, chink | by the root of wholesome merit | prediction of awakening | will obtain  
`dārike` (girl), root of wholesome merit, you will obtain a prediction of `bodhi` (awakening).

50.2 yat tvayā tasya bhikṣo rūpaliṅgasaṃsthā na dṛṣṭā bhaviṣyasi tvaṃ dārike anāgate 'dhvani acintyair aparimāṇaiḥ kalpair nagaradhvajo nāma tathāgato 'rhan samyaksaṃbuddho vidyācaraṇasaṃpannaḥ sugato lokavid anuttaraḥ puruṣadamyasārathiḥ śāstā devānāṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ ca buddho bhagavān* loke |

that | by you | of that | of the bhikṣo | form, mark, and appearance | not | seen | will become | you | dārikā rent, chink | in the future | in time | by inconceivable | by immeasurable | after aeons | Nagaradhvaja | by name | Tathāgata | arhan | Samyaksaṃbuddha | endowed with knowledge and conduct | Sugata | lokavid | unsurpassed | puruṣadamyasārathi | Śāstṛ | of deva | and | of humans | and | Buddha | Bhagavān | in the world  
Because the `rūpa` (form), marks, and bearing of that bhikṣo were seen by you, you will become, `dārike` (girl), in future time, after inconceivable, immeasurable aeons, a Tathāgata named Nagaradhvaja in the loka, arhan, Samyaksaṃbuddha, endowed with knowledge and conduct, Sugata, lokavid, unsurpassed puruṣadamyasārathi, Śāstṛ of devas and humans, Buddha, Bhagavān."

50.3 atha sā dārikā bhagavantaṃ triḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvaivam āha |  
⏏️(atha) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(tad) 🚺⨀①(dārikā) 🚹⨀②(bhagavat) ⏏️(tris) ⏏️(pradakṣiṇam) 🔽(tvā)(karoti) ⏏️(evam) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)

Then that girl, having circumambulated Bhagavān three times to the right, spoke thus:

"Of what sort, Bhagavan, will my Buddha field be, where indeed I shall become a Buddha?"

50.5 bhagavān āha |  
🚹⨀①(bhagavat) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
Bhagavān | said  
Bhagavān said:

🚺⨀①🆎(aparimita+guṇasaṃcaya) ⏏️(nāma) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(tad) 🚻⨀①🆎(buddha+kṣetra) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(bhavati)  
Aparimitaguṇasaṃcaya | by name | that | Buddha field | will be  
"That Buddha field will be named Aparimitaguṇasaṃcaya.

50.7 yādṛśī ca sā sukhāvatī lokadhātuḥ tādṛśaṃ tad buddhakṣetraṃ bhaviṣyati |  
🚺⨀①(yādṛśa) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(tad) 🚺⨀①(sukhāvatī) 🚹⨀①🆎(loka+dhātu) 🚻⨀①(tādṛśa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(tad) 🚻⨀①🆎(buddha+kṣetra) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(bhavati)  
of such sort as | and | that | Sukhāvatī | world system system | of such sort | that | Buddha field | will be  
And just as that lokadhātu system of Sukhāvatī is, such will that Buddha field be.

50.8 paryaṅkaniṣaṇṇā āryopapādukā bodhisatvā bhaviṣyanti |  
🚹⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(paryaṅka+niṣaṇṇa) 🚹⨂①🆎(ārya+upapāduka) 🚹⨂①(bodhisattva) 🟢⨂🤟⏭(bhavati)  
seated cross-legged | liberated and spontaneously born | bodhisattvas | will be  
There will be spontaneously born ārya bodhisattvas seated cross-legged.

50.9 īdṛśaṃ tad buddhakṣetram* |  
🚻⨀①(īdṛśa) *️⃣🤟🚻⨀①(tad) 🚻⨀①🆎(buddha+kṣetra)  
such | that | Buddha field  
Of such a sort will that Buddha field be."

50.10 (Asv 113)  
  
  
(Asv 113)

51.1 atha ca sā dārikā tuṣṭā udagrā āttamanāḥ pramuditā prītisaumasyajātā svagṛhagamanam ārabdhā |  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(ca) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀①(tad) 🚺⨀①(dārikā) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(tuṣṭa) 🚺⨀①(udagra) 🚺⨂①(āttamana) 🚺⨀①(pramudita) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(prīti+sauma+sya+jāta) 🚻⨀②🆎(sva+gṛha+gamana) 🚺⨀①🔽(ta)(ārabdha)  
then | and | that | girl | satisfied | uplifted | elated | delighted | filled with joy and gladness | motion to her own house | began  
And then that girl, satisfied, elated, delighted, overjoyed, filled with joy and gladness, set out for her own house.

51.2 atha bhagavāṃs tāṃ dārikām evam āha |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) *️⃣🤟🚺⨀②(tad) 🚺⨀②(dārikā) ⏏️(evam) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
then | Bhagavān | that | girl | thus | said  
Then Bhagavān spoke thus to that girl:

51.3 tvaṃ dārike saptame divase kālaṃ kariṣyasi |  
*️⃣🤘⚧⨀①(yuṣmad) 🚺⨀⓪(dārikā) 🚹⨀⑦(saptama) 🚹⨀⑦(divasa) 🚹⨀②(kāla) 🟢⨀🤘⏭(karoti)  
you | dārikā rent, chink | on the seventh | on the day | time | will make  
"You, `dārike` (girl), will pass away on the seventh day.

51.4 kālaṃ kṛtvā |  
🚹⨀②(kāla) 🔽(tvā)(karoti)  
time | having made  
Having passed away—

51.5 pūrvasyān diśi magadhaviṣaye rājā ajitaseno nāma |  
🚹⨀⑤🆎(pūrva+sya) 🚺⨀⑦(diś) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(magadha+viṣaya) 🚹⨀①(rājan) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+jita+sena) ⏏️(nāma)  
in the eastern | in the direction | in the country of Magadha | king | Ajitasena | by name  
in the eastern direction, in the country of Magadha, there is a king named Ajitasena.

51.6 tasya rājño 'jitasenasya antaḥpurasahasram asti |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(tad) 🚹⨀⑥(rājan) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(na+jita+sena) ⏏️(antar) 🚻⨀①🆎(pura+sa+hasra) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(asti)  
of that | of the king | of Ajitasena | inner | thousand of the palace | exists  
There are a thousand women in the inner quarters of that King Ajitasena.

51.7 tasya khalu punā rājño 'jitasenasya putro janiṣyase |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑥(tad) ⏏️(khalu) 🚺⨀①(punā) 🚹⨀⑥(rājan) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(na+jita+sena) 🚹⨀①(putra) 🔵⨀🤘⏭(jāyate)  
of that | indeed | moreover | of the king | of Ajitasena | son | will be born  
Indeed you will be born as the son of that very King Ajitasena.

51.8 eṣa eva tava paścimo garbhavāso bhaviṣyati |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(etad) ⏏️(eva) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀⑥(yuṣmad) 🚹⨀①(paścima) 🚹⨀①🆎(garbha+vāsa) 🟢⨀🤟⏭(bhavati)  
this | indeed | your | last | dwelling in a womb | will be  
This very one will be your last dwelling in a womb."

51.9 atha bhagavān paścimakena nagaradvāreṇa śrāvastyā mahānagaryā niṣkrānto yena jetavanaṃ vihāras tenopasaṃkrāntaḥ |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(bhagavat) 🚻⨀③🔽(ka)🆎(paścima+ka) 🚻⨀③🆎(nagara+dvāra) 🚺⨀①🔽(ya)(śrāvastya) 🚺⨀①🆎(mahā+nagarī) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(niṣkrānta) ⏏️(yena) 🚹⨀②🆎(jeta+vana) 🚹⨀①(vihāra) ⏏️(tena) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(upasaṃkrānta)  
then | Bhagavān | by the western | by the city gate | from Śrāvastī | from the great city | departed | where | Jetavana | monastery | there | approached  
Then Bhagavān departed from the great city of Śrāvastī by the western city gate and approached where the Jetavana monastery was.

51.10 athāyuṣmān ānando bhagavantaṃ dūrata evāgacchantaṃ dṛṣṭvā pādau śirasābhivandya triḥpradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhagavantaṃ gāthābhir adhyabhāṣata |

then | āyuṣmān | Ānanda | Bhagavān | from afar | indeed | coming | having seen | two feet | with the head | having paid homage to | having circumambulated three times | Bhagavān | with verses | addressed  
Then āyuṣmān Ānanda, seeing Bhagavān coming from afar, having bowed with his head at his feet and circumambulated him three times to the right, addressed Bhagavān in gāthās.

52.1 suvarṇavarṇaṃ varalakṣaṇārcitaṃ  
🚹⨀②🆎(suvarṇa+varṇa) 🚹⨀②🔽(ta)🆎(vara+lakṣaṇa+arcita)

🚹⨂①(dvi) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+viṃśati+lakṣaṇa+rūpa+dhārin)  
two | bearer of twenty characteristics and form

52.3 yadā tvayā āgatu piṇḍapātikā  
⏏️(yadā) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀③(yuṣmad) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(āgata) 🚹⨂①🆎(piṇḍa+pātika)  
when | by you | come | alms-food collector

52.4 vimocayitvā iha sarvasatvā //  
🔽(tvā)(vimocayati) ⏏️(iha) 🚹⨀①🆎(sarva+satvan)  
having liberated | here | all beings

Golden-coloured, adorned with supreme `lakṣaṇa` (characteristics),  
bearing a form with twenty-two characteristics,  
when you have come back as a piṇḍapātika,  
having released all beings here.

the power of `maitrī` (goodwill) has been extended by you to the entire world.

and the world has been released by you from fear.

there will be the ruin of the teaching;

"Listen, Ānanda. Those sattvas who in the last kāla, in the last age, after my final extinguishment, when the ruin of the `saddharma` (True Dharma) is underway, at the kāla of the disappearance of the True Dharma, in villages, towns, market-towns, districts, kingdoms, and royal capitals.

60.1 ye satvā [vā] araṇyāyatane gaṇḍyākoṭanaśabdaṃ śroṣyanti namo buddhāyeti kariṣyanti teṣāṃ pañcānantaryāṇi karmāṇi parikṣayaṃ (Asv 115) yāsyanti |

Or those beings who in a forest abode shall hear the sound of the striking of the gong and say 'Homage to the Buddha', their five karmans of immediate retribution will go to complete exhaustion.

60.2 īdṛśāny ānanda gaṇḍīśabdasya kuśalamūlāni |

Of such a sort, Ānanda, are the roots of wholesome merit of the sound of the gong."

Then āyuṣmān Ānanda calmly and serenely struck the gong.

⏏️(yathā) ⏏️(yathā) 🚻⨀⑦(āsana) 🚹⨂①🔽(ta)(niṣaṇṇa) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṇḍa+pāta) 🚹⨂①🔽(at)(paribhuñjat) ⏏️(sma)  
as | as | on the seat | seated | alms-food-food | partook of | (past)  
Seated each on his respective seat, they partook of the piṇḍapāta-food.

60.6 atha tatraiva śrāvakamadhye nandimitro nāma mahāśrāvakaḥ sannipatito 'bhūt* sanniṣaṇṇaḥ |

61.3 atha nandimitro mahāśrāvakaḥ pratyūṣakālasamaye suvarṇavarṇaṃ vastraṃ prāvṛtya yena pūrvasyān diśi magadhaviṣaye rājño 'jitasenasya rājadhānī tenānukrānto 'bhūt* |

Then Nandimitra the mahāśrāvaka, at dawn time, having put on a golden-coloured garment, went along toward where the royal capital of King Ajitasena was, in the country of Magadha in the eastern direction.

I eat that food which you give,  
savoury food and drink, supreme flavourful drink;  
indeed you will become free from `kleśa` (defilements),  
having gained the very rare attainment of human birth.

65.1 sudurlabhaṃ śāsanu nāyakasya

Very rare is the teaching of the nāyaka;  
supreme serenity of `śraddhā` (confidence) is very rare;  
and those bhikṣus who have gone forth in the teaching—  
very rare is the teaching of the nāyakas.

Very rare is the sight of the supreme Sugata;  
homage be to you, Buddha, mahānubhāva;  
homage be to you, consisting of Dharma, `mahāmune` (great sage);  
homage be to you, ārya discerning of defilements.

82.1 atha sa rājā ajitaseno nandimitraṃ mahāśrāvakaṃ guṇavarṇam udīrayitvā tūṣṇīṃ sthito 'bhūt* |  
⏏️(atha) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀①(tad) 🚹⨀①(rājan) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+jita+sena) 🚹⨀②🆎(nandi+mitra) 🚹⨀②🆎(mahā+śrāvaka) 🚹⨀②🆎(guṇa+varṇa) 🔽(tvā)(udīrayati) ⏏️(tūṣṇīm) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(sthita) 🟢⨀🤟⏮(bhavati)  
then | that | king | Ajitasena | Nandimitra | mahāśrāvaka | praise of qualities | having uttered | silently | stood | became  
Then that King Ajitasena, having proclaimed the praise of the qualities of Nandimitra the mahāśrāvaka, stood silent.

82.2 atha nandimitro mahāśrāvako (Asv 119) rājānam ajitasenam evam āha |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①🆎(nandi+mitra) 🚹⨀①🆎(mahā+śrāvaka) 🚹⨀②(rājan) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+jita+sena) ⏏️(evam) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
then | Nandimitra | mahāśrāvaka | king | Ajitasena | thus | said  
Then Nandimitra the mahāśrāvaka spoke thus to King Ajitasena:

82.3 evam astu mahārāja bhuñje piṇḍapātaṃ tava gṛhe |  
⏏️(evam) 🟢⨀🤟⏹(asti) 🚹⨀⓪(mahārāja) 🟢⨀👆▶️(bhunakti) 🚹⨀②🆎(piṇḍa+pāta) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀⑥(yuṣmad) 🚻⨀⑦(gṛha)

"So be it, mahārāja; I shall eat piṇḍapāta in your house."

82.4 atha rājā ajitasenaḥ khādanīyena bhojanīyena taṃ nandimitramahāśrāvakaṃ santarpayati sma |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(rājan) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+jita+sena) 🚻⨀③🔽(anīya)(khādanīya) 🚻⨀③(bhojanīya) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(tad) 🚹⨀②🆎(nandi+mitra+mahā+śrāvaka) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(saṃtarpayati) ⏏️(sma)  
then | king | Ajitasena | with hard food | with soft food | that | Nandimitra mahāśrāvaka | satisfies | (past)  
Then King Ajitasena satisfied that Nandimitra the mahāśrāvaka with hard and soft food.

82.5 atha nandimitramahāśrāvako rājānam ajitasenam evam āha |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①🆎(nandi+mitra+mahā+śrāvaka) 🚹⨀②(rājan) 🚹⨀②🆎(na+jita+sena) ⏏️(evam) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
then | Nandimitra mahāśrāvaka | king | Ajitasena | thus | said  
Then Nandimitra the mahāśrāvaka spoke thus to King Ajitasena:

82.6 kiṃ tava mahārāja asmin pṛthivīpradeśe udyānabhūmir asti |  
⏏️(kim) *️⃣🤘⚧⨀⑥(yuṣmad) 🚹⨀⓪(mahārāja) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀⑦(idam) 🚹⨀⑦🆎(pṛthivī+pradeśa) 🚹⨀①🔽(at)(udyāt) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+bhūmi) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(asti)  
whether | of you | mahārāja | in this | in region of earth | park | ground | exists

82.7 rājā āha |  
🚹⨀①(rājan) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
king | said

there is | mahāśrāvaka | park | ground | of me | delightful | very beautiful

82.9 nandimitra āha |  
🚹⨀①🆎(nandi+mitra) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
Nandimitra | said  
Nandimitra said:

🟢⨀👆▶️(gacchati) *️⃣👆⚧⨀①(asmad) 🚹⨀⓪(mahārāja)  
I go | I | mahārāja  
"I am going, mahārāja;

82.11 tām udyānabhūmiṃ prekṣe |  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨀②(tad) 🚻⨀②🔽(at)(udyāt) 🚺⨀②🆎(na+bhūmi) 🟢⨀👆▶️(prekṣate)  
that | park | ground | I look at  
I shall look at that park ground."

82.12 rājā āha |  
🚹⨀①(rājan) 🟢⨀🤟🔄(āha)  
king | said  
The king said:

82.13 gaccha nandimitra |  
🟢⨀🤘⏹(gacchati) 🚹⨀⓪🆎(nandi+mitra)  
go | of an attendant of Śiva, TĀr  
"Go, Nandimitra;

82.14 udyānabhūmiṃ prekṣasva |  
🚻⨀②🔽(at)(udyāt) 🚺⨀②🆎(na+bhūmi) 🔵⨀🤘⏹(prekṣate)  
park | ground | look at  
look at the park ground."

82.15 atha nandimitramahāśrāvako yena rājño 'jitasenasyodyānabhūmis tenopasaṃkrāntaḥ |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①🆎(nandi+mitra+mahā+śrāvaka) ⏏️(yena) 🚹⨀⑥(rājan) 🚹⨀⑥🆎(na+jita+sena) 🚹⨀①🔽(at)(udyāt) 🚺⨀①🆎(na+bhūmi) ⏏️(tena) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(upasaṃkrānta)  
then | Nandimitra mahāśrāvaka | where | of the king | of Ajitasena | park | ground | there | approached  
Then Nandimitra the mahāśrāvaka approached where the park ground of King Ajitasena was.

82.16 atha tatrodyānabhūmau ye udyānaguṇās te sarve santīti |  
⏏️(atha) ⏏️(tatra) 🚹⨀①🔽(at)(udyāt) 🚺⨀⑦🆎(na+bhūmi) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(yad) 🚹⨀①🔽(at)(udyāt) 🚹⨂①🆎(na+guṇa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(tad) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(sarva) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(asti) ⏏️(iti)  
then | there | park | in the ground | which | park | qualities | those | all | exist | thus  
Now in that park ground all the qualities of a park were present:

82.17 yā graiṣmikyaḥ puṣkariṇyas tāḥ śītalajalaparipūrṇā yā vārṣikyas tā nātyuṣṇā nātiśītalajalaparipūrṇāḥ |  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(yad) 🚹⨀①🔽(ya)(graiṣmikya) 🚺⨂①(puṣkariṇī) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(tad) 🚺⨂①🆎(śītala+jala+paripūrṇa) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(yad) 🚺⨂①(vārṣikī) *️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(tad) ⏏️(na) 🚺⨂①(atyuṣṇa) ⏏️(na) 🚺⨂①🆎(atiśītala+jala+paripūrṇa)  
which | summer | lotus pools | those | complete with cool water | which | rainy-season | those | not | too hot | not | complete with too cool water  
the summer lotus ponds were completely filled with cool water, and the rainy-season ponds were completely filled with water neither too hot nor too cold.

82.18 tāś ca puṣkariṇyaḥ suvarṇasopānasaṃcchannā divyā ramaṇīyāḥ |  
*️⃣🤟🚺⨂①(tad) ⏏️(ca) 🚺⨂①(puṣkariṇī) 🚺⨂①🆎(suvarṇa+sopāna+saṃchanna) 🚺⨂①(divya) 🚺⨂①🔽(anīya)(ramaṇīya)  
those | and | lotus pools | covered with golden steps | heavenly | delightful  
And those lotus ponds were covered with golden steps, heavenly and delightful.

82.19 ye ca jāṃbūpakaparibhogaguṇās te sarve santīti |  
*️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(yad) ⏏️(ca) 🚹⨀①(jāṃba) 🚹⨂①🆎(upaka+paribhoga+guṇa) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(tad) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂①(sarva) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(asti) ⏏️(iti)  
which | and | Jambudvīpa | qualities of enjoyment | those | all | exist | thus  
And all the qualities of enjoyment in Jambudvīpa were present:

82.20 adhimuktakacampakāśokamucilindapāṭalasumanāsaugandhikapuṣpāṇi santīti |  
🚹⨂①🆎(adhimuktaka+campaka) 🚻⨂①🆎(na+śoka+mucilinda+pāṭala+sumanā+saugandhika+puṣpa) 🟢⨂🤟▶️(asti) ⏏️(iti)  
adhimuktaka and campaka | aśoka, mucilinda, pāṭala, sumanā, and saugandhika flowers | exist | thus  
there were adhimuktaka, campaka, aśoka, mucilinda, pāṭala, sumanā, and saugandhika flowers.

He entered it with his hair in one place, his eyes in another, his teeth in another, his neck in another, his arms in another, his heart in another, his belly in another, his thighs in another, his calves in another, and his feet in another.

do not experience such feelings.  
For having killed yourself, you will go to hell;  
and even to the very dreadful Raurava hell you will go.

and he has not been slain by yakṣas, nor by ghosts, nor by rākṣasas;  
this one is indeed a bodhisattva in the loka, a releaser from old age and illness.

this one is worthy of offerings in the loka, bringing `sukha` (happiness) to all beings.

they will not go to a bad destination, being bound for rebirth in the heavenly world.

then | that | prince | thus | said

"Look, mahārāja, this bhikṣu has entered into a bodhisattva focus; completely free from all defilements, a Bhavasāgarapāraṃgata, for the benefit of all existences he points out the auspicious mārga."

115.7 ahaṃ saptame divase jetavanaṃ nāma vihāraṃ gamiṣyāmi śākyamunes tathāgatasyārhataḥ samyaksaṃbuddhasya darśanāya vandanāya paryupāsanāya dharmaśravaṇāya (Asv 127) |

On the seventh day I shall go to the monastery named Jetavana to see, pay homage to, attend upon, and hear the Dharma from Śākyamuni, the Tathāgata, arhataḥ, Samyaksaṃbuddha."

Then King Ajitasena, seeing the form, complexion, marks, and bearing of Bhagavān, fainted and fell down on the surface of the earth.

inconceivable hundreds of tens of millions of births,  
never before have I seen such a form here:  
you are the lokanātha, supreme worthy of offerings.

You are the caravan leader, releaser from old age and illness.

who | of the lokanātha | indeed | form | sees

whoever indeed sees the rūpa of the lokanātha.

"Go, mahārāja, to your own house; on the seventh day I shall come."

122.7 atha rājā ajitasenas tuṣṭa udagra āttamanāḥ pramuditaḥ prītisaumansyajātas taṃ kalyāṇamitraṃ bhikṣuṃ purataḥsthāpya svagṛhaṃ gatvā sarvān amātyān āmantrayate sma |  
⏏️(atha) 🚹⨀①(rājan) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+jita+sena) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)(tuṣṭa) 🚹⨀①(udagra) 🚹⨂①(āttamana) 🚹⨀①(pramudita) 🚹⨀①🔽(ta)🆎(prīti+sa+oman+sya+jāta) *️⃣🤟🚹⨀②(tad) 🚻⨀②🆎(kalyāṇa+mitra) 🚹⨀②(bhikṣu) ⏏️(puratas) 🔽(ya)(sthāpayati) 🚻⨀②🆎(sva+gṛha) 🔽(tvā)(gacchati) *️⃣🤟🚹⨂②(sarva) 🚹⨂②(amātya) 🔵⨀🤟▶️(āmantrati) ⏏️(sma)  
then | king | Ajitasena | pleased | joyful | elated | delighted | filled with joy and gladness | that | spiritual friend | bhikṣu | in front | having placed | own house | having gone | all | ministers | addresses | (past)  
Then King Ajitasena, satisfied, elated, delighted, overjoyed, and filled with joy and gladness, placing that kalyāṇamitra bhikṣu in front and going to his own house, addressed all the ministers:

122.8 sarvair grāmanagaranigamajanapadaiḥ pathaṃ śodhayitavyaṃ gṛhe gṛhe dhvajāny ucchrāpitavyāni gṛhe gṛhe ratnamayāni (Asv 129) kumbhāni paripūrayitavyāni |

"By all the villages, towns, market-towns, and districts the road should be cleared; at house after house banners should be raised; at house after house jewelled pitchers should be filled completely."

122.9 tadā taiḥ sarvair amātyair ājñātam* |

then | by those | by all | by the ministers | ordered  
Then it was commanded by all those ministers.

122.10 sarvagrāmanagaranigamajanapadaiḥ pathaṃ śodhitaṃ dhvajāny ucchrāpitāni ratnamayāni kumbhāni paripūritāni |  
🚹⨂③🆎(sarva+grāma+nagara+nigama+jana+pada) 🚻⨀①(patha) 🚻⨀①🔽(ta)(śodhita) 🟢⨀👆⏹(dhvajati) 🚹⨂①(ucchrā) 🚻⨂①🔽(ta)🆎(na+pita) 🚻⨂①(ratnamaya) 🚻⨂①(kumbha) 🚻⨂①(paripūrita)  
by all villages, cities, market towns, and provinces | road | cleared | banners | raised | raised | jewelled | jars | filled  
By all the villages, towns, market-towns, and districts the road was cleared, banners were raised, and jewelled pitchers were filled completely.

122.11 yatra rājā ajitasenaḥ prativasati tatra dvādaśakoṭyo dhvajānām ucchrāpitā dvādaśakoṭyo ratnamayānāṃ kumbhānāṃ paripūritāḥ |  
⏏️(yatra) 🚹⨀①(rājan) 🚹⨀①🆎(na+jita+sena) 🟢⨀🤟▶️(prativasati) ⏏️(tatra) 🚺⨂①🆎(dvādaśa+koṭi) 🚹⨂⑥(dhvaja) 🚹⨂①(ucchrā) 🚺⨂①🔽(tā)(apitā) 🚺⨂①🆎(dvādaśa+koṭi) 🚻⨂⑥(ratnamaya) 🚻⨂⑥(kumbha) 🚺⨂①(paripūrita)

Where King Ajitasena resided, there one hundred and twenty million banners were raised and one hundred and twenty million jewelled pitchers were filled completely.

122.12 yāvat saptame divase tathāgato 'rhan samyaksaṃbuddhaḥ śāriputramaudgalyāyanānandapūrṇamaitrāyaṇīputrapramukhair mahāśrāvakasaṃghaiḥ parivṛtaḥ puraskṛtaḥ ajitasenasya rājño rājadhānīm anuprāptaḥ |

until | on the seventh | on the day | Tathāgata | arhan | Samyaksaṃbuddha | Śāriputramaudgalyāyana | headed by Ānanda and Pūrṇa Maitrāyaṇīputra | by assemblies of great disciples | surrounded | placed in front | of Ajitasena | of the king | royal capital | arrived  
In due course, on the seventh day, the Tathāgata, arhan, Samyaksaṃbuddha, surrounded and attended by saṃghas of mahāśrāvakas headed by Śāriputra, Maudgalyāyana, Ānanda, and Pūrṇa Maitrāyaṇīputra, arrived at the royal capital of King Ajitasena.

Then King Ajitasena, taking a basket of flowers and placing his kalyāṇamitra in front.

Then he was satisfied by King Ajitasena with abundant nourishment, with hard and soft food.

the attainment of going forth, praised by the Sugata!

go forth swiftly, do not delay, tāta.

The very rare attainment of human birth has been gained.

and having gone forth swiftly, indeed I have gained.

Then the king, having heard his son's words.
